   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is PENDING.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CONFIRMED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status != PENDING, return false
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->

      }
    }

    return false;

   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.customer, Booking.reservations, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, List, EDate
   * 5. Input: f : Flight - Flight to book.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp.
   *          listOfPassengerNames : List<String> - Names of passengers traveling together.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if booking and all reservations created successfully; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: f is open for booking. Current time precedes departure. No duplicate names in list. No passenger in the list already has a reservation on f.
   * 8. Post-condition: New Booking is created and linked to customer. Multiple Reservations are generated with unique IDs and PENDING status, linked to the booking and flight. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if f.openForBooking is true
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    // Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a matching passenger name
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassengerName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Instantiate new Booking object, set customer to this, and add to this.bookings
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    this.getBookings().add(booking);

    // For each name in listOfPassengerNames: call booking.createReservation(f, name, now)
    int reservationIndex = 0;
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames) {
      Reservation reservation = booking.createReservation(f, name, now);
      // Assign a unique ID
      reservation.setReservationID(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      // Initialize with status PENDING
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    }

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Confirms an existing pending reservation belonging to the customer.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID.
   *    - If not found, return false.
   *    - Retrieve the associated flight f.
   *    - Check if now < f.departureTime. If false, return false.
   *    - Check if f.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
   *    - If reservation.status != PENDING, return false.
   *    - Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.reservations, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate
   * 5. Input: reservationID : String - Unique ID of the reservation to confirm.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp for validation.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if confirmation successful; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is PENDING.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CONFIRMED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status != PENDING, return false
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to the customer.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID.

  {

      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();

            return false;

          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.

   *    - Check if f.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.

   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, List, EDate

   *          listOfPassengerNames : List<String> - Names of passengers traveling together.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if booking and all reservations created successfully; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: f is open for booking. Current time precedes departure. No duplicate names in list. No passenger in the list already has a reservation on f.
   * 8. Post-condition: New Booking is created and linked to customer. Multiple Reservations are generated with unique IDs and PENDING status, linked to the booking and flight. Returns true.

   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"

  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if f.openForBooking is true
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    // Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a matching passenger name
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassengerName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Instantiate new Booking object, set customer to this, and add to this.bookings
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    this.getBookings().add(booking);

    // For each name in listOfPassengerNames: call booking.createReservation(f, name, now)
    int reservationIndex = 0;
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames) {
      Reservation reservation = booking.createReservation(f, name, now);
      // Assign a unique ID
      reservation.setReservationID(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      // Initialize with status PENDING
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    }

    return true;
  }

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->

   *    - Return true.

  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status != PENDING, return false
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to the customer.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID.

   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is not already CANCELED.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CANCELED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT

  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID

      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled)

            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CANCELED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;

   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp.
   *          listOfPassengerNames : List<String> - Names of passengers traveling together.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if booking and all reservations created successfully; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: f is open for booking. Current time precedes departure. No duplicate names in list. No passenger in the list already has a reservation on f.
   * 8. Post-condition: New Booking is created and linked to customer. Multiple Reservations are generated with unique IDs and PENDING status, linked to the booking and flight. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if f.openForBooking is true
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    // Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a matching passenger name
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassengerName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Instantiate new Booking object, set customer to this, and add to this.bookings
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    this.getBookings().add(booking);

    // For each name in listOfPassengerNames: call booking.createReservation(f, name, now)
    int reservationIndex = 0;
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames) {
      Reservation reservation = booking.createReservation(f, name, now);
      // Assign a unique ID
      reservation.setReservationID(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      // Initialize with status PENDING
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    }

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Confirms an existing pending reservation belonging to the customer.

  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {

    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status != PENDING, return false
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to the customer.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID.
   *    - If not found, return false.

   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CANCELED. Returns true.

  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {

    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled)
          if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CANCELED) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CANCELED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

   *    - Set reservation.status = CANCELED.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.reservations, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate
   * 5. Input: reservationID : String - Unique ID of the reservation to cancel.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp for validation.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if cancellation successful; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is not already CANCELED.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CANCELED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled)
          if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CANCELED) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CANCELED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.

  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if f.openForBooking is true
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    // Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a matching passenger name
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassengerName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    }

      return false;

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.

5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   *    - Return true.

   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, List, EDate

   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp.
   *          listOfPassengerNames : List<String> - Names of passengers traveling together.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if booking and all reservations created successfully; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: f is open for booking. Current time precedes departure. No duplicate names in list. No passenger in the list already has a reservation on f.
   * 8. Post-condition: New Booking is created and linked to customer. Multiple Reservations are generated with unique IDs and PENDING status, linked to the booking and flight. Returns true.

  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if f.openForBooking is true
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    // Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a matching passenger name
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassengerName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Instantiate new Booking object, set customer to this, and add to this.bookings
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    this.getBookings().add(booking);

    // For each name in listOfPassengerNames: call booking.createReservation(f, name, now)
    int reservationIndex = 0;
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames) {
      Reservation reservation = booking.createReservation(f, name, now);
      // Assign a unique ID
      reservation.setReservationID(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      // Initialize with status PENDING
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    }

    return true;
  }

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Confirms an existing pending reservation belonging to the customer.

   *    - Return true.

  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status != PENDING, return false
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  /**

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->

  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)

      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true

            return false;

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if f.openForBooking is true
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    // Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a matching passenger name
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassengerName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Instantiate new Booking object, set customer to this, and add to this.bookings
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    this.getBookings().add(booking);

    int reservationIndex = 0;
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames) {
      Reservation reservation = booking.createReservation(f, name, now);
      // Assign a unique ID
      reservation.setReservationID(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      // Initialize with status PENDING
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    }

            return false;
          }

          }

            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED

          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Initiates a new booking for a customer on a flight with multiple passengers, performing global and local validations.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates. If duplicates found, return false.
   *    - Check if f.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
   *    - Check if now < f.departureTime. If false, return false.
   *    - Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a passenger name matching any name in listOfPassengerNames. If conflict, return false.
   *    - Instantiate new Booking object, set customer to this, and add to this.bookings.
   *    - For each name in listOfPassengerNames: call booking.createReservation(f, name, now). Each created reservation must be assigned a unique ID and initialized with status PENDING.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.customer, Booking.reservations, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, List, EDate
   * 5. Input: f : Flight - Flight to book.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp.
   *          listOfPassengerNames : List<String> - Names of passengers traveling together.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if booking and all reservations created successfully; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: f is open for booking. Current time precedes departure. No duplicate names in list. No passenger in the list already has a reservation on f.
   * 8. Post-condition: New Booking is created and linked to customer. Multiple Reservations are generated with unique IDs and PENDING status, linked to the booking and flight. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if f.openForBooking is true
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if now < f.departureTime
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    // Query f.reservations to ensure no existing reservation has a matching passenger name
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassengerName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Instantiate new Booking object, set customer to this, and add to this.bookings
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    this.getBookings().add(booking);

    // For each name in listOfPassengerNames: call booking.createReservation(f, name, now)
    int reservationIndex = 0;
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames) {
      Reservation reservation = booking.createReservation(f, name, now);
      // Assign a unique ID
      reservation.setReservationID(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      // Initialize with status PENDING
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    }

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Confirms an existing pending reservation belonging to the customer.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID.
   *    - If not found, return false.
   *    - Retrieve the associated flight f.
   *    - Check if now < f.departureTime. If false, return false.
   *    - Check if f.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
   *    - If reservation.status != PENDING, return false.
   *    - Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.reservations, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate
   * 5. Input: reservationID : String - Unique ID of the reservation to confirm.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp for validation.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if confirmation successful; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is PENDING.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CONFIRMED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status != PENDING, return false
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to the customer.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID.
   *    - If not found, return false.
   *    - Retrieve the associated flight f.
   *    - Check if now < f.departureTime. If false, return false.
   *    - Check if f.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
   *    - If reservation.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled).
   *    - Set reservation.status = CANCELED.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.reservations, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate
   * 5. Input: reservationID : String - Unique ID of the reservation to cancel.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp for validation.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if cancellation successful; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is not already CANCELED.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CANCELED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getReservationID())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled)
          if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CANCELED) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CANCELED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // Validate listOfPassengerNames contains no internal duplicates
    Set<String> seenNames = new HashSet<>(listOfPassengerNames);
    if (seenNames.size() != listOfPassengerNames.size()) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {

    }

    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (listOfPassengerNames.contains(existingRes.getPassenger().getName())) {
        return false;

   *    - Check if f.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
   *    - If reservation.status != PENDING, return false.
   *    - Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.reservations, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate
   * 5. Input: reservationID : String - Unique ID of the reservation to confirm.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp for validation.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if confirmation successful; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is PENDING.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CONFIRMED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getId())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status != PENDING, return false
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to the customer.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID.
   *    - If not found, return false.
   *    - Retrieve the associated flight f.
   *    - Check if now < f.departureTime. If false, return false.
   *    - Check if f.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
   *    - If reservation.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled).
   *    - Set reservation.status = CANCELED.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Customer.bookings, Booking.reservations, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * 4. Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate
   * 5. Input: reservationID : String - Unique ID of the reservation to cancel.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp for validation.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if cancellation successful; false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Reservation exists and belongs to this customer. Flight has not departed and is still open for booking. Status is not already CANCELED.
   * 8. Post-condition: Reservation status is updated to CANCELED. Returns true.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // Traverse this.bookings and their respective reservations to locate the reservation with id == reservationID
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(r.getId())) {
          // Retrieve the associated flight f
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          
          // Check if now < f.departureTime
          if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Check if f.openForBooking is true
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // If reservation.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled)
          if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CANCELED) {
            return false;
          }
          
          // Set reservation.status = CANCELED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // If not found, return false
    return false;
  }

   * 1. Summary: Retrieves a list of flights matching specified origin, destination, and departure date.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Initialize empty result list.
   *    - For each flight in this.flights:
   *      - Check if origin matches any city served by flight.departureAirport or flight.departureAirport.id.
   *      - Check if dest matches any city served by flight.arrivalAirport or flight.arrivalAirport.id.
   *      - Check if flight.departureTime.date() equals date.date().
   *      - If all match, add to result list.
   *    - Return result list.
   * 3. Related Features: Airline.flights, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime, Airport.servesForCities
   * 4. Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
   * 5. Input: origin : String - Name or ID of departure city/airport.
   *           dest : String - Name or ID of arrival city/airport.
   *           date : EDate - Target departure date.
   * 6. Output: List<Flight> - Matching flights. Empty list if none found.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Strings are non-empty. Date is valid.
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns filtered list. Original airline state remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<>();
    Calendar searchCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    searchCal.setTime(date);
    
    for (Flight fl : getFlights()) {
        Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
        depCal.setTime(fl.getDepartureTime());
        
        boolean dateMatch = (depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == searchCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH));
                             
        boolean originMatch = fl.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin);
        if (!originMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getDepartureAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(origin)) {
                originMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        boolean destMatch = fl.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest);
        if (!destMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getArrivalAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(dest)) {
                destMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        if (dateMatch && originMatch && destMatch) {
            result.add(fl);
        }
    }
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Retrieves a list of flights matching specified origin, destination, and departure date.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Initialize empty result list.
   *    - For each flight in this.flights:
   *      - Check if origin matches any city served by flight.departureAirport or flight.departureAirport.id.
   *      - Check if dest matches any city served by flight.arrivalAirport or flight.arrivalAirport.id.
   *      - Check if flight.departureTime.date() equals date.date().
   *      - If all match, add to result list.
   *    - Return result list.
   * 3. Related Features: Airline.flights, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime, Airport.servesForCities
   * 4. Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
   * 5. Input: origin : String - Name or ID of departure city/airport.
   *           dest : String - Name or ID of arrival city/airport.
   *           date : EDate - Target departure date.
   * 6. Output: List<Flight> - Matching flights. Empty list if none found.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Strings are non-empty. Date is valid.
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns filtered list. Original airline state remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<>();
    Calendar searchCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    searchCal.setTime(date);
    
    for (Flight fl : getFlights()) {
        Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
        depCal.setTime(fl.getDepartureTime());
        
        boolean dateMatch = (depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == searchCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH));
                             
        boolean originMatch = fl.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin);
        if (!originMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getDepartureAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(origin)) {
                originMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        boolean destMatch = fl.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest);
        if (!destMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getArrivalAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(dest)) {
                destMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        if (dateMatch && originMatch && destMatch) {
            result.add(fl);
        }
    }
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Retrieves a list of flights matching specified origin, destination, and departure date.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Initialize empty result list.
   *    - For each flight in this.flights:
   *      - Check if origin matches any city served by flight.departureAirport or flight.departureAirport.id.
   *      - Check if dest matches any city served by flight.arrivalAirport or flight.arrivalAirport.id.
   *      - Check if flight.departureTime.date() equals date.date().
   *      - If all match, add to result list.
   *    - Return result list.
   * 3. Related Features: Airline.flights, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime, Airport.servesForCities
   * 4. Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
   * 5. Input: origin : String - Name or ID of departure city/airport.
   *           dest : String - Name or ID of arrival city/airport.
   *           date : EDate - Target departure date.
   * 6. Output: List<Flight> - Matching flights. Empty list if none found.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Strings are non-empty. Date is valid.
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns filtered list. Original airline state remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<>();
    Calendar searchCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    searchCal.setTime(date);
    
    for (Flight fl : getFlights()) {
        Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
        depCal.setTime(fl.getDepartureTime());
        
        boolean dateMatch = (depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == searchCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH));
                             
        boolean originMatch = fl.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin);
        if (!originMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getDepartureAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(origin)) {
                originMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        boolean destMatch = fl.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest);
        if (!destMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getArrivalAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(dest)) {
                destMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        if (dateMatch && originMatch && destMatch) {
            result.add(fl);
        }
    }
    return result;
  }

        depCal.setTime(fl.getDepartureTime());

                destMatch = true;
            }
        }

            result.add(fl);
        }
    }
    return result;

   *          passenger : String - Name of the passenger.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp used for validation checks.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if the reservation is successfully created and added to the booking; false if validation fails (flight already departed or passenger already booked on this flight).

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Retrieves a list of flights matching specified origin, destination, and departure date.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Initialize empty result list.
   *    - For each flight in this.flights:
   *      - Check if origin matches any city served by flight.departureAirport or flight.departureAirport.id.
   *      - Check if dest matches any city served by flight.arrivalAirport or flight.arrivalAirport.id.
   *      - Check if flight.departureTime.date() equals date.date().
   *      - If all match, add to result list.
   *    - Return result list.
   * 3. Related Features: Airline.flights, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime, Airport.servesForCities
   * 4. Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
   * 5. Input: origin : String - Name or ID of departure city/airport.
   *           dest : String - Name or ID of arrival city/airport.
   *           date : EDate - Target departure date.
   * 6. Output: List<Flight> - Matching flights. Empty list if none found.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Strings are non-empty. Date is valid.
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns filtered list. Original airline state remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<>();
    Calendar searchCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    searchCal.setTime(date);
    
    for (Flight fl : getFlights()) {
        Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
        depCal.setTime(fl.getDepartureTime());
        
        boolean dateMatch = (depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == searchCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH));
                             
        boolean originMatch = fl.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin);
        if (!originMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getDepartureAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(origin)) {
                originMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        boolean destMatch = fl.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest);
        if (!destMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getArrivalAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(dest)) {
                destMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        if (dateMatch && originMatch && destMatch) {
            result.add(fl);
        }
    }
    return result;
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Retrieves a list of flights matching specified origin, destination, and departure date.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Initialize empty result list.
   *    - For each flight in this.flights:
   *      - Check if origin matches any city served by flight.departureAirport or flight.departureAirport.id.
   *      - Check if dest matches any city served by flight.arrivalAirport or flight.arrivalAirport.id.
   *      - Check if flight.departureTime.date() equals date.date().
   *      - If all match, add to result list.
   *    - Return result list.
   * 3. Related Features: Airline.flights, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime, Airport.servesForCities
   * 4. Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
   * 5. Input: origin : String - Name or ID of departure city/airport.
   *           dest : String - Name or ID of arrival city/airport.
   *           date : EDate - Target departure date.
   * 6. Output: List<Flight> - Matching flights. Empty list if none found.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Strings are non-empty. Date is valid.
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns filtered list. Original airline state remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<>();
    Calendar searchCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    searchCal.setTime(date);
    
    for (Flight fl : getFlights()) {
        Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
        depCal.setTime(fl.getDepartureTime());
        
        boolean dateMatch = (depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == searchCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH));
                             
        boolean originMatch = fl.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin);
        if (!originMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getDepartureAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(origin)) {
                originMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        boolean destMatch = fl.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest);
        if (!destMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getArrivalAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(dest)) {
                destMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        if (dateMatch && originMatch && destMatch) {
            result.add(fl);
        }
    }
    return result;
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Retrieves a list of flights matching specified origin, destination, and departure date.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Initialize empty result list.
   *    - For each flight in this.flights:
   *      - Check if origin matches any city served by flight.departureAirport or flight.departureAirport.id.
   *      - Check if dest matches any city served by flight.arrivalAirport or flight.arrivalAirport.id.
   *      - Check if flight.departureTime.date() equals date.date().
   *      - If all match, add to result list.
   *    - Return result list.
   * 3. Related Features: Airline.flights, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime, Airport.servesForCities
   * 4. Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
   * 5. Input: origin : String - Name or ID of departure city/airport.
   *           dest : String - Name or ID of arrival city/airport.
   *           date : EDate - Target departure date.
   * 6. Output: List<Flight> - Matching flights. Empty list if none found.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Strings are non-empty. Date is valid.
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns filtered list. Original airline state remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<>();
    Calendar searchCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    searchCal.setTime(date);
    
    for (Flight fl : getFlights()) {
        Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
        depCal.setTime(fl.getDepartureTime());
        
        boolean dateMatch = (depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == searchCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.MONTH) &&
                             depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH) == searchCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH));
                             
        boolean originMatch = fl.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin);
        if (!originMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getDepartureAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(origin)) {
                originMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        boolean destMatch = fl.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest);
        if (!destMatch) {
            EList<String> cities = fl.getArrivalAirport().getServesForCities();
            if (cities != null && cities.contains(dest)) {
                destMatch = true;
            }
        }
                        
        if (dateMatch && originMatch && destMatch) {
            result.add(fl);
        }
    }
    return result;
  }

   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Validate temporal constraint: if now >= f.departureTime, return false.
   *    - Validate passenger uniqueness: iterate through f.reservations; if any existing reservation's passenger name matches the input passenger string, return false.
   *    - Generate a unique reservation ID (e.g., UUID/GUID).
   *    - Instantiate a new Passenger object with the given passenger name.
   *    - Instantiate a new Reservation with the generated ID, the new Passenger object, f as the flight reference, and status set to PENDING.
   *    - Add the new Reservation to this.reservations.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Booking.reservations
   * 4. Related Classes: Reservation, Flight, Passenger
   * 5. Input: f : Flight - The target flight for the reservation.
   *          passenger : String - Name of the passenger.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp used for validation checks.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if the reservation is successfully created and added to the booking; false if validation fails (flight already departed or passenger already booked on this flight).
   * 7. Pre-condition: f is a valid Flight object. now represents the current system time. passenger is a valid, non-null string.
   * 8. Post-condition: On success, a new Reservation with status PENDING is appended to this.reservations. On failure, the system state remains unchanged and false is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
  {
    // Validate temporal constraint: if now >= f.departureTime, return false.
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
      return false;
    }

    // Validate passenger uniqueness: iterate through f.reservations; if any existing reservation's passenger name matches the input passenger string, return false.
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (passenger != null && existingRes.getPassenger() != null && passenger.equals(existingRes.getPassenger().getName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Generate a unique reservation ID (e.g., UUID/GUID).
    String id = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    // Instantiate a new Passenger object with the given passenger name.
    Passenger newPassenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    newPassenger.setName(passenger);

    // Instantiate a new Reservation with the generated ID, the new Passenger object, f as the flight reference, and status set to PENDING.
    Reservation newReservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
    newReservation.setId(id);
    newReservation.setPassenger(newPassenger);
    newReservation.setFlight(f);
    newReservation.setStatus(FlightsPackage.eINSTANCE.getReservationStatus().getLiterals().get("PENDING"));

    // Add the new Reservation to this.reservations.
    this.getReservations().add(newReservation);

    // Return true.
    return true;
  }

  { ... /* omitted */ }

  { ... /* omitted */ }

  public Customer basicGetCustomer()
  { ... /* omitted */ }

  public void setCustomer(Customer newCustomer)
  { ... /* omitted */ }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Creates a pending reservation for a specific passenger on a flight within this booking context. Validates flight timing and passenger uniqueness before creation.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - Validate temporal constraint: if now >= f.departureTime, return false.
   *    - Validate passenger uniqueness: iterate through f.reservations; if any existing reservation's passenger name matches the input passenger string, return false.
   *    - Generate a unique reservation ID (e.g., UUID/GUID).
   *    - Instantiate a new Passenger object with the given passenger name.
   *    - Instantiate a new Reservation with the generated ID, the new Passenger object, f as the flight reference, and status set to PENDING.
   *    - Add the new Reservation to this.reservations.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: Booking.reservations
   * 4. Related Classes: Reservation, Flight, Passenger
   * 5. Input: f : Flight - The target flight for the reservation.
   *          passenger : String - Name of the passenger.
   *          now : EDate - Current timestamp used for validation checks.
   * 6. Output: boolean - true if the reservation is successfully created and added to the booking; false if validation fails (flight already departed or passenger already booked on this flight).
   * 7. Pre-condition: f is a valid Flight object. now represents the current system time. passenger is a valid, non-null string.
   * 8. Post-condition: On success, a new Reservation with status PENDING is appended to this.reservations. On failure, the system state remains unchanged and false is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
  {
    // Validate temporal constraint: if now >= f.departureTime, return false.
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
      return false;
    }

    // Validate passenger uniqueness: iterate through f.reservations; if any existing reservation's passenger name matches the input passenger string, return false.
    for (Reservation existingRes : f.getReservations()) {
      if (passenger != null && existingRes.getPassenger() != null && passenger.equals(existingRes.getPassenger().getName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Generate a unique reservation ID (e.g., UUID/GUID).
    String id = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    // Instantiate a new Passenger object with the given passenger name.
    Passenger newPassenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    newPassenger.setName(passenger);

    // Instantiate a new Reservation with the generated ID, the new Passenger object, f as the flight reference, and status set to PENDING.
    Reservation newReservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
    newReservation.setId(id);
    newReservation.setPassenger(newPassenger);
    newReservation.setFlight(f);
    newReservation.setStatus(FlightsPackage.eINSTANCE.getReservationStatus().getEEnumLiteral("PENDING"));

    // Add the new Reservation to this.reservations.
    this.getReservations().add(newReservation);

    // Return true.
    return true;
  }
